        Idea:
Consolidate the media-lightbox persistence architecture. Replace 3 competing custom persistence hooks (useMediaPersistence + useEditSettingsSync + useEditSettingsPersistence) with a single hook using the app's standard useAutoSaveSettings pattern. Fix the useLayoutEffect dependency array landmine. Flatten the 6-7 level hook nesting. Preserve the 3-way context split (Canvas/Form/Status).

User notes and answers:
- User accepts the variant-level prompt regression. Keep a lightweight in-memory-only Map in useStrokePersistence that caches prompt/numGenerations alongside strokes per variant key, restoring them on variant switch. No localStorage or DB for this - just session-level scratch buffer. This addresses flag-3 without scope creep.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

        Idea:
Consolidate the media-lightbox persistence architecture. Replace 3 competing custom persistence hooks (useMediaPersistence + useEditSettingsSync + useEditSettingsPersistence) with a single hook using the app's standard useAutoSaveSettings pattern. Fix the useLayoutEffect dependency array landmine. Flatten the 6-7 level hook nesting. Preserve the 3-way context split (Canvas/Form/Status).

User notes and answers:
- User accepts the variant-level prompt regression. Keep a lightweight in-memory-only Map in useStrokePersistence that caches prompt/numGenerations alongside strokes per variant key, restoring them on variant switch. No localStorage or DB for this - just session-level scratch buffer. This addresses flag-3 without scope creep.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 2 of 6.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T3']
        - Already completed task IDs available as dependency context: ['T1', 'T2']

        Actionable tasks for this batch:
        [
  {
    "id": "T3",
    "description": "Phase 3a: Slim useMediaPersistence to stroke-only and rename.         Idea:
Consolidate the media-lightbox persistence architecture. Replace 3 competing custom persistence hooks (useMediaPersistence + useEditSettingsSync + useEditSettingsPersistence) with a single hook using the app's standard useAutoSaveSettings pattern. Fix the useLayoutEffect dependency array landmine. Flatten the 6-7 level hook nesting. Preserve the 3-way context split (Canvas/Form/Status).

User notes and answers:
- User accepts the variant-level prompt regression. Keep a lightweight in-memory-only Map in useStrokePersistence that caches prompt/numGenerations alongside strokes per variant key, restoring them on variant switch. No localStorage or DB for this - just session-level scratch buffer. This addresses flag-3 without scope creep.

        Idea:
Consolidate the media-lightbox persistence architecture. Replace 3 competing custom persistence hooks (useMediaPersistence + useEditSettingsSync + useEditSettingsPersistence) with a single hook using the app's standard useAutoSaveSettings pattern. Fix the useLayoutEffect dependency array landmine. Flatten the 6-7 level hook nesting. Preserve the 3-way context split (Canvas/Form/Status).

User notes and answers:
- User accepts the variant-level prompt regression. Keep a lightweight in-memory-only Map in useStrokePersistence that caches prompt/numGenerations alongside strokes per variant key, restoring them on variant switch. No localStorage or DB for this - just session-level scratch buffer. This addresses flag-3 without scope creep.

        Idea:
Consolidate the media-lightbox persistence architecture. Replace 3 competing custom persistence hooks (useMediaPersistence + useEditSettingsSync + useEditSettingsPersistence) with a single hook using the app's standard useAutoSaveSettings pattern. Fix the useLayoutEffect dependency array landmine. Flatten the 6-7 level hook nesting. Preserve the 3-way context split (Canvas/Form/Status).

User notes and answers:
- User accepts the variant-level prompt regression. Keep a lightweight in-memory-only Map in useStrokePersistence that caches prompt/numGenerations alongside strokes per variant key, restoring them on variant switch. No localStorage or DB for this - just session-level scratch buffer. This addresses flag-3 without scope creep.
